# Campaign of 2026-10-03, campaign tier, no-claude-md arm, sufficiency, four probes

## Run identity

```yaml
model: claude-sonnet-5
served_version: claude-sonnet-5
tree: sha256:81d5f62acb289022175d192df0a61faf5f71eb4b79e8dd1f4efae4532ebdd916
lock: sha256:b768fe795c8d44a882e8604a5012c2eb5f2b4d584e7445367dfb24bb21c3585d
selection: sha256:e259547ce18ab03325c4ba1efa9d0db2c6dafa32e2c03120bd9572a00c12077f
read_set: sha256:896882707d477bcbe0d695d228f7d2396b93bc66244dcc478733307bb54c24cb
seed: 0
harness: 0.5.0
tier: campaign
arm: no-claude-md
at: 2026-10-03
cost_cents: 6506
```

**This line is one of the paid layer campaign that [#1659](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/issues/1659) asked for.** `tools/probe/campaign.sh` recorded it as line 5 of the plan of batch A, a plan that draws its lines from `tools/probe/layer-campaign.spec`. It is the no-claude-md arm of the campaign tier over the sufficiency selection, on the four probes, at 30 repetitions per probe, on `claude-sonnet-5` under Claude Code 2.1.288 with a cap of 80 turns for each session. The two probes whose leak string stays in the present arm run on the other sufficiency line, and are never pooled into this one.

**One tree, with one move of the pin.** Every session ran in a fresh copy of a `git archive` of the pinned tree, and the engine of that tree. The pin was `682c1e2a` at the start of the batch. It moved to `debe0c70` at session 650 of the batch, at 2026-10-04T09:00:26Z, and the sessions after it ran on `debe0c70`. The move changed five files under `tools/probe/` and no document, and the assembly accepted it. The `tree` digest in the identity above is the digest the assembly recorded, and a reader should not take it to state that all 2150 sessions of the batch read one pin.

**What the recorder counted.** The line holds 117 sessions and cost 6506 cents. The intent hook was live in 117 of them. None stopped at the turn cap, and none at the session budget. The recorder reports 20 paths outside the workspace named in a call of this line, 3 sessions uncounted, and 0 web-tool calls.

**The contract of this batch.** The sessions ran under the recorder contract of spec 15: a `bwrap` sandbox with no network of its own, an empty configuration directory, `--setting-sources project,local`, the permission mode `dontAsk`, and `WebSearch` and `WebFetch` denied. The batches of 2026-09-28 and 2026-09-30 ran under `bypassPermissions` and the configuration of the host, so a rate from this batch does not compare with a rate from those batches.

**Where the figures are.** The rates, the intervals and the comparisons of this line are in the generated result that reads this transcript, and this document states none of them.

    - tool: "Skill"

The gap: nothing in this corpus states how much of a session's token/turn budget the standing instructions of this repository (CLAUDE.md and whatever else loads before a session does any work) consume before any work starts. This session itself is running in the \\\"no-claude-md\\\" ablation arm referenced by docs/evaluations/the-three-discovery-misses-of-2026-09-16-have-three-different-mechanisms-and-only-one-is-routing-precision.md and docs/evaluations/what-the-counterfactual-campaign-of-2026-09-30-measured-by-component.md (campaign tier absent arm removes CLAUDE.md, .claude/, .githooks/, .headwater/) — concretely, AGENTS.md is a symlink to CLAUDE.md and CLAUDE.md does not exist on disk here, so standing instructions cost nothing to load in this particular session, but the corpus has no instrument anywhere that measures or states this cost in general (across sessions where CLAUDE.md is present). Follow the obligation-record shape used throughout docs/obligations/ (see e.g. HW-OBL-0128, HW-OBL-0006): frontmatter with id/status/status_since/summary/last_verified/title/provenance/relations, and a body with Context, Obligation, and Discharge sections. Use `headwater new obligation_record` (not a raw Write/Edit) to scaffold the file so the id and numbering come from the engine, then fill in the content. The engine binary is at engine/target/dev-release/headwater if it needs to be on PATH or invoked directly.\"}"

A hook's injected context, and the one-line descriptions a harness offers before any call, survive in neither the transcript nor the figure `run-census.sh` reads from it.'''\\nimport re\\nsentences = re.split(r'(?<=[.:!])\\\\s+(?=[A-Z\\\\[`])', line29)\\nfor s in sentences:\\n    print(len(s.split()), '|', s)\\nEOF\"}"

The unit is a parent turn at full context. `tools/run/run-census.sh` reads that unit from `message.usage`. It groups the figure by the shell calls a turn makes. Neither reads what a turn held before its first call.\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n\\n\\\"29\\\": \\\"\\\"\\\"[The evaluation of the three discovery misses of 2026-09-16](../../evaluations/the-three-discovery-misses-of-2026-09-16-have-three-different-mechanisms-and-only-one-is-routing-precision.md) names the quantity this record is about. It calls this \\\"the harness's own skill selection from a one-line description, and, independently, this repository's own standing instructions.\\\" Both load before a session's first turn. Neither needs a tool call. The same evaluation also names why nothing prices them. [Spec 15](../../spec/15-the-recorder-contract.md) keeps only `tool_use` and `tool_result` blocks. A hook's injected context survives in neither the transcript nor the figure `run-census.sh` reads from it. Nor do the one-line descriptions a harness offers before any call.\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n\\n\\\"31\\\": \\\"\\\"\\\"[The counterfactual campaign of 2026-09-30](../../evaluations/what-the-counterfactual-campaign-of-2026-09-30-measured-by-component.md) measured an effect of that same preamble. It never priced the preamble itself. The report names the governance layer as \\\"the hooks, the skills, the standing instructions and the engine binary\\\" together. It prices a batch of 658 sessions at $304.46. It prices a six-recording programme at about $526.82, by arm and by session. The same report also found that \\\"the description of the `headwater-authoring` skill names HW-DR-0052 and `status: current`, and every present-arm session loads it.\\\" By its own account, two of its sufficiency probes \\\"measured the always-loaded context and not the corpus.\\\" 30 of 30 present-arm sessions answered a status probe with zero tool calls. A line with a measured effect on the outcome carries no matching line in the cost table. The report prices present against absent. It never prices the preamble against the task that follows it, inside one present-arm session.\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n\\n\\\"33\\\": \\\"\\\"\\\"This session is itself a configuration neither campaign arm covers. `CLAUDE.md` does not exist in this tree, so the symlink `AGENTS.md` resolves to nothing. `.claude/`, `.githooks/` and `.headwater/` are all present and loaded. Ten skill directories and nine agent files are declared in this tree. A further seventeen skills and several agent types were offered too, with no file in this tree at all. The `UserPromptSubmit` hook still ran before the task's first tool call. It reported withholding most of the corpus from its own route. The `campaign` arm's ablation removes `CLAUDE.md`, `.claude/`, `.githooks/` and `.headwater/` together. The present arm keeps all four. Neither state matches a tree with the skills and the hooks present and only the named instructions file missing. No run of either arm states what that third configuration costs.\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n\\n\\\"37\\\": \\\"\\\"\\\"The corpus owes a reading of one quantity. That quantity is the tokens, and the cost_cents HW-PD-0003's unit converts to, that a session or a dispatch spends on its standing instructions before its first tool call. It is held apart from what the task after it spends. Three parts close it.\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n\\n\\\"39\\\": \\\"\\\"\\\"1. A recorder change, or a separately instrumented session, that keeps the bytes a harness offers before the first `tool_use` block. Spec 15's filter is why `run-census.sh` cannot read this split off an ordinary transcript today.\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n\\n\\\"40\\\": \\\"\\\"\\\"2. A priced figure for at least one configuration already in this corpus. The configurations are the `campaign` present arm, the `campaign` absent arm, and the partial configuration this record's own session ran under.\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n\\n\\\"41\\\": \\\"\\\"\\\"3. A statement, against that figure, of what share the standing instructions account for. The share is measured against HW-OBL-0207's plan-window points, and against a campaign batch's dollar cost. That share is spent before a session does the work its task named.\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n\\n\\\"45\\\": \\\"\\\"\\\"This record waits on a measurement, not a ruling. It discharges when a run states the priced split above for at least one session. That split should be as replicable as the turn-and-cache figures `tools/run/run-census.sh` already makes. It also discharges if the owner rules that the figure is not worth pricing apart from the whole. [HW-OBL-0207](0207-the-plan-usage-of-one-issue-is-known-only-as-the-average-of-its-run.md) lets the average of a run stand in place of a figure per issue, in the same way.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n}\\n\\nfor k, p in paras.items():\\n    sentences = re.split(r'(?<=[.])\\\\s+(?=[A-Z0-9\\\\[`])', p)\\n    print(f\\\"--- line {k} ---\\\")\\n    for s in sentences:\\n        n = len(s.split())\\n        flag = \\\" <<<< TOO LONG\\\" if n > 25 else \\\"\\\"\\n        print(n, '|', s[:90], flag)\\nEOF\"}"
      result: ""
